alternating between sockets; SMT siblings are only\n// used once every core has been handed out.\nstatic std::vector<int> buildCpuOrder() {\n    cpu_set_t mask;\n    CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n    if (buckets[smt].size() <= pkg) {\n            buckets[smt].resize(pkg + 1);\n        }\n        buckets[smt][pkg].push_back(cpu);\n    }\n\n    std::vector<int> order;\n    for (auto& perPackage : Summary of the changes (all in the two existing files):\n\n**`black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp`**\n- The pricing loop is now `#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)`. Each option is independent and costs the same, so a static schedule gives perfect load balance with zero scheduling overhead, and results are written to distinct indices — bit-identical output to the serial version.\n- Option generation runs under the *same* static schedule and thread count, and the input/result buffers moved from `std::vector` to default-initialized `new T[]` arrays so no page is touched by the master thread first. Each thread therefore first-touches exactly the pages it later reads/writes, which keeps the data NUMA-local on this dual-socket machine.\n- Added topology-aware pinning (`bindThreads`): libgomp leaves threads unbound by default, and migration destroyed the first-touch locality — that alone cost ~50% of throughput. It reads the process affinity mask plus sysfs sibling/package info and pins thread *t* so that consecutive threads land on distinct physical cores alternating between sockets, using SMT siblings only after every core is taken. It is skipped entirely if the user set `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`OMP_PLACES`, respects `taskset` masks, and is `#ifdef __linux__`-guarded.\n- Thread count is capped at `numOptions / 4096` so tiny inputs aren't dominated by the cost of waking 256 threads (the default `-n 10000` case got slower otherwise).\n- `validateResults`/`generateOptions` now take pointers; `print_results` gets a vector built after the timed region, so output formatting is unchanged.\n\n**`black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt`**: `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` + link `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. Executable name unchanged, no new dependencies.\n\nMeasured on the 2×64-core EPYC 7763 (256 threads), `-n 20000000`:\n\n| config | options/s | speedup |\n|---|---|---|\n| original serial | 10.8 M | 1× |\n| OpenMP, 64 threads | 729 M | 67× |\n| OpenMP, 256 threads | 1.48–1.72 G | ~137–158× |\n\nScaling is near-linear to 128 threads (116×) with a further ~29% from SMT. Result hashes match the serial reference exactly for n = 0, 1, 7, 9999, 1M, 2M (16-core taskset) and 20M, and `-v` validation output is unchanged."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":271,"cache_read_input_tokens":52776,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":271},"output_tokens":5,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"10f6599c-764e-467e-9705-1ec2db6a76d4","uuid":"2139a382-d786-488a-a6e6-1f9269f1ca50","timestamp":"2026-09-06T19:01:41.623Z","request_id":"req_011CenhhogUmzrvMixN5eBqE"}

Each option is independent and costs the same, so a static schedule gives perfect load balance with zero scheduling overhead, and results are written to distinct indices — bit-identical output to the serial version.\n- Option generation runs under the *same* static schedule and thread count, and the input/result buffers moved from `std::vector` to default-initialized `new T[]` arrays so no page is touched by the master thread first. It reads the process affinity mask plus sysfs sibling/package info and pins thread *t* so that consecutive threads land on distinct physical cores alternating between sockets, using SMT siblings only after every core is taken. It is skipped entirely if the user set `OMP_PROC_BIND`/`OMP_PLACES`, respects `taskset` masks, and is `#ifdef __linux__`-guarded.\n- Thread count is capped at `numOptions / 4096` so tiny inputs aren't dominated by the cost of waking 256 threads (the default `-n 10000` case got slower otherwise).\n- `validateResults`/`generateOptions` now take pointers; `print_results` gets a vector built after the timed region, so output formatting is unchanged.\n\n**`black-scholes/CMakeLists.txt`**: `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED)` + link `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.
